# Work Index — Legendary Arena (Slim Edition)

> **Slim build of WORK_INDEX.md** — collapsed completed WPs to one-liners;
> full entries preserved only for incomplete or deferred WPs.
> The full version lives in the git history on main.
>
> **Location note:** This file lives at `docs/ai/work-packets/WORK_INDEX.md`.
> All references in the coordination system (00.1, CLAUDE.md) point here.

---

## Format

```
- [ ] WP-NNN — Short Title — [pending | in-progress | blocked: reason]
- [x] WP-NNN — Short Title — Completed YYYY-MM-DD
```

**Rules:**
- One Work Packet per Claude Code session — never combine two packets in one session
- Packets must be executed in dependency order unless explicitly noted as parallel
- A packet may not be executed until all listed dependencies are complete
- Status is updated only when the packet's `## Definition of Done` is fully met


---

## Review Status Legend

| Mark | Meaning |
|------|---------|
| ✅ Reviewed | Packet has been audited: SharePoint links removed, all required sections present, verified against conventions |
| ⚠️ Needs review | Packet has NOT been audited — likely contains SharePoint links, missing Definition of Done, `.mjs` test paths |

All existing WPs through WP-060 are marked ✅ Reviewed. WP-061 through WP-065 were drafted 2026-04-16 and passed lint-gate review. Any future WPs must be reviewed before Claude Code executes them.

---

## Foundation Prompts (run once before Work Packets begin)

These are execution prompts, not Work Packets. They establish the deployment
environment that all Work Packets build on top of. Run them in the order shown.

| Prompt | Description | Status |
|--------|-------------|--------|
| `00.4` | Connection & environment health check | ✅ complete 2026-04-09 |
| `00.5` | R2 data & image validation | ✅ complete 2026-04-09 |
| `01` | Render.com backend — server, schema, `render.yaml` | ✅ complete 2026-04-09 |
| `02` | Database migration runner + `data/migrations/` | ✅ complete 2026-04-09 |

Run `00.4` first. Fix any failures before proceeding. Then run `00.5`, `01`, `02`
in that order. When all four pass, WP-002 is unblocked.

- [x] WP-107 — Profile Integrity / Anti-Cheat Surface. **Done** 2026-05-24. Server-only addition shipping three admin-only endpoints under `/api/admin/players/:handle/` (`GET /integrity`, `POST /suspend`, `POST /unsuspend`) plus the shared `requireUnsuspendedAccount(database, accountId): Result<void>` intake helper at `apps/server/src/auth/requireUnsuspendedAccount.ts` (ships `Library-only` — first caller deferred to the future score-submission request-handler WP per RS-1 Option A scope-out 2026-05-23; the score-submission HTTP route does not exist at HEAD per api-endpoints.md:193). Migration 015 adds `is_suspended BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT FALSE` to `legendary.players` + creates `legendary.admin_actions` append-only audit table with DB-level `CHECK (action_type IN ('suspend','unsuspend'))`, `CHECK (length(reason) BETWEEN 1 AND 500)`, FKs to `legendary.players(ext_id) ON DELETE RESTRICT` on both account-id columns, + composite index `admin_actions_target_idx (target_account_id, created_at DESC, action_id DESC)`. Transaction ownership locked in `adminProfile.logic.ts` (route handlers contain zero transaction-control literals — grep-verified); every successful mutation executes `BEGIN -> UPDATE legendary.players -> INSERT legendary.admin_actions RETURNING action_id -> COMMIT` with the audit INSERT completing BEFORE COMMIT; injected-fault tests prove any step's failure rolls back BOTH writes. Race-safe unconditional `UPDATE legendary.players SET is_suspended = $1 WHERE ext_id = $2` (NOT read-modify-write — concurrent duplicate writes acceptable; idempotency DB-enforced). `GET /integrity` composes profile + audit-log reads inside one `BEGIN ISOLATION LEVEL REPEATABLE READ ... COMMIT` so the response cannot show stale suspension state alongside fresh audit rows. Audit-log tail bound `LIMIT 100`, `ORDER BY created_at DESC, action_id DESC` (action_id tiebreaker resolves same-millisecond collisions deterministically). `AdminProfileResponse` locked to 4 admin-only fields (`accountId`, `handle`, `isSuspended`, `recentAuditLog`); `AuditLogEntry` 5 fields; `AdminActionRequest` 1 field (`reason`); `AdminActionResponse` 2 fields (`ok: true`, `actionId: string`). Closed-union `AdminPlayerActionType = 'suspend' | 'unsuspend'`; closed-union error codes `'unauthorized' | 'forbidden' | 'not_found' | 'invalid_request' | 'internal_error'`. Self-action forbidden at the route layer (`actingAccountId === targetAccountId` after handle -> ext_id resolution returns 400 `{ code: 'invalid_request', reason: 'Admins cannot suspend their own account.' }` with **zero audit rows written** — verified by test). Reason `.trim()`-normalized BEFORE validation; trimmed length 1-500 chars; whitespace-only rejected at the application boundary (DB CHECK is defense-in-depth). `requireUnsuspendedAccount` HTTP error mapping locked as caller-contract for the future score-submission request-handler WP: `'suspended'` -> 403 `{ code: 'forbidden', reason: 'Account is suspended.' }`; `'lookup_failed'` -> 500 `{ code: 'internal_error' }`. WP-107 is the FIRST caller of `requireAdminSession` (WP-159's helper, shipped 2026-05-17). Bidirectional drift-detection tests on `ADMIN_PROFILE_ERROR_CODES` + `ADMIN_PLAYER_ACTION_TYPES` + `REQUIRE_UNSUSPENDED_ACCOUNT_ERROR_CODES`. Locked contract files byte-identical pre/post: `adminSession.ts` (WP-159), `adminGate.ts` (WP-110), `sessionToken.{logic,types}.ts` (WP-112), `auth/hanko/**` (WP-126); no touch to `apps/server/src/{competition,leaderboards,par}/` (Option A scope-out — `git diff --name-only` empty for those paths). Pre-existing `join-match.test.ts` failure (1 fail, unrelated to WP-107 scope) carried forward — same disposition as WP-159 / WP-163. Server test baseline 397/330/1/66 -> 441/374/1/66 (+44 new: 8 requireUnsuspendedAccount + 19 logic + 17 routes). `pnpm -r build` exits 0. Catalog updated per §21 + D-11804: 3 new `Wired` rows + 1 new `Library-only` row (replace-whole-row semantics). All 6 Open Questions LOCKED per RS-1/RS-2 (2026-05-23): Q1 N/A under Option A; Q2 NO public-read-block; Q3 STAY historical scores; Q4 NO auto-leave teams; Q5 indefinite retention; Q6 single WP. 01.5 NOT INVOKED (no engine surface). 01.6 post-mortem authored (first admin-mutation surface; audit-log primitive becomes a new code category). Hard-deps: WP-052, WP-101, WP-102, WP-104, WP-112, WP-126, WP-131, WP-053, WP-105, **WP-159** — all ✅. D-10701 (account-level scope), D-10702 (audit log append-only single-table), D-10703 (handle in URL, not accountId). 11 files (post-Option-A). See [WP-107](WP-107-profile-integrity-anti-cheat-surface.md) + [EC-195](../execution-checklists/EC-195-profile-integrity-anti-cheat-surface.checklist.md) + [01.6 post-mortem](../post-mortems/01.6-WP-107-profile-integrity-anti-cheat-surface.md).

- [x] WP-192 — Hanko JWKS Refresh Interval Parsing Guard (Server). **Done** 2026-05-31 (EC-219). Server-only, single-file surgical hardening of `apps/server/src/server.mjs`'s `tryConstructHankoVerifier()` env-parse boundary. Replaced single-step `Number(refreshIntervalRaw)` with a two-step parse + `Number.isFinite()` guard so malformed `HANKO_JWKS_REFRESH_INTERVAL_MS` shapes (typo, empty string, `'123abc'`, `'Infinity'`, `'-Infinity'`, `'NaN'`) collapse to `undefined` → factory's D-12603 default-substitution branch (300 000 ms) fires — instead of `NaN` propagating into `setInterval(..., NaN)` and the WHATWG spec coercing it to 1 ms (hammering Hanko's JWKS endpoint ~1000 req/sec between deploy and rollback). **Origin:** drafted during WP-176's execution session 2026-05-24 when a production deploy log surfaced `refresh=NaNms`; parked as a stash with the note "ride along with next server.mjs touch with an EC prefix"; 7 days later no server.mjs WP had absorbed it, so WP-192 landed it under proper EC governance. **Executed:** single-block edit at lines ~224-244 of `server.mjs` (`envComplete` gate, dev/prod diagnostics, D-13104 masked-URL logging, `refreshLogged` ternary, and `createHankoSessionVerifier` call shape are byte-identical pre/post). Behavior preservation for valid inputs: a numeric env value (e.g., `"60000"`) produces the same `jwksRefreshIntervalMs = 60000` and same `refresh=60000ms` log line as before; an unset env var still produces `undefined` → `refresh=defaultms`. Only malformed shapes change behavior. **Test coverage deferred (D-19202)** — no tests added or modified; server test baseline preserved byte-identical at **400 pass / 0 fail / 66 skipped**. Test addition would require either stubbing `createHankoSessionVerifier` (invasive module-level mocking) or extracting the parse into a pure helper (`parseRefreshIntervalMs(rawValue): number | undefined`) and unit-testing it directly; both expand scope beyond the surgical guard the parked stash represented. The rewritten `// why:` block (citing D-12603, WHATWG `setInterval(..., NaN)` → 1 ms coercion, 2026-05-24 production log, and the closed malformed-shape list) + production log evidence carry the contract. **Commit-prefix discipline:** WP-192 exists as a full WP rather than a standalone `INFRA:` commit because the local commit-msg hook Rule 5 + CI `ec-code-traceability` job both reject non-`EC-###:` prefixes for `apps/` changes. `pnpm -r build` exits 0. D-19201..D-19202 landed. Hard-deps: WP-131 ✅ (verifier startup guard), WP-126 ✅ (factory). 5 files (1 source + 4 governance). See [WP-192](WP-192-hanko-jwks-refresh-interval-guard.md) + [EC-219](../execution-checklists/EC-219-hanko-jwks-refresh-interval-guard.checklist.md).

Two same-layer pipeline fixes WP-167 left latent: (A) `applySchemeDeckCounts` assigns each count **independently** (omitted ⇒ engine default; no `undefined` key written) so twist-only entries are first-class; (B) `apply-card-counts.mjs` gains the same scheme-deck-count overlay + exact-slug loud-fail for the 4 outlier sets (`2099`/`amwp`/`wpnx`/`wtif`), which otherwise silently ignore any entry (corrects a false `_note` claim). All 40 `data/cards/*.json` regenerated; only scheme twist/bystander deltas; idempotent. **Carve-out:** player-count-dependent / additive-per-player / non-constant twist counts (e.g. Civil War 2–3p:8 / 4–5p:5; `dstr` 2p:9 / 1·4p:10 / 3·5p:11) are NOT encoded — a single int can't represent them and WP-168 reads a single int; they keep the 8 fallback pending a future per-player-mapping schema+engine WP. No schema change (fields exist, D-16702), no engine change (WP-168 consumes). Hard-dep: WP-167 ✅. Independent of WP-168. New `schema.schemeDeckCounts.test.ts`. D-16803 (independent counts + outlier-applier parity; relaxes the WP-167 "both required" note), D-16804 (conditional-twist carve-out; finalized list landed at execution). Plugs the four ext_id grammars WP-168 introduced (villain copies `{setAbbr}-villain-{groupSlug}-{cardSlug}-{NN}` per D-16802; generic Master Strikes `master-strike-{NN}` per D-16801) and the two pre-WP-168 grammars that were never registered (`scheme-twist-{schemeSlug}-{NN}`, `bystander-villain-deck-{NN}`) into `G.cardDisplayData` so the UIState projection's `resolveDisplay` no longer falls through to `UNKNOWN_DISPLAY_PLACEHOLDER` (the literal `<unknown>` name surfaced by the RevealOverlay popup on every Master Strike / Scheme Twist / city-villain reveal in production at `play.legendary-arena.com`). Bystander uses two-tier lookup that **prefers `slug === 'bystander'` over positional `bystanders[0]`** (msp1 / vill / wtif carry the generic entry at non-zero positions; cvwr / ssw2 / xmen carry only named characters — tier-2 picks `bystanders[0]` as the acknowledged-imperfect fallback for those sets; dstr has empty array → tier-3 literal). No `core`-set fallback for bystanders (identity is per-scheme). New 3rd parameter `numPlayers: number` on `buildCardDisplayData` for the bystander-count fallback per D-1412 (`MatchSetupConfig` does not carry `numPlayers`; mirrors `villainDeck.setup.ts`'s `context.ctx.numPlayers` read; explicit `// why:` calls out that `matchConfig.bystandersCount` is a DIFFERENT concept — rescue-pile supply, not villain-deck virtual cards); single call-site update in `buildInitialGameState.ts`. New cross-builder superset invariant test (`Object.keys(G.cardDisplayData)` ⊇ `[...villainDeck.deck, ...villainDeck.discard]`) — the regression guard that would have caught the original WP-168 gap. The shared `performVillainReveal` body is unchanged, so scheme-twist chained reveals (`schemeTwistResolvers.ts:230,491` call it directly) intentionally bypass the guard. Move never throws (early `return` per Move Validation Contract); no `G.messages` entry for the blocked reveal (silent no-op, mirroring the existing stage-gate early return). Implementation requires re-exports in `packages/game-engine/src/index.ts` (sweep.analyze.ts has zero `node:*` imports, so adding to the Runtime-Safe Engine Surface is layer-safe per D-14401). 11-line type-only + 3-fn re-export addition; zero runtime impact; satisfies AC #2 literally. **Build:** `pnpm -r build` exits 0. **Tests:** `pnpm --filter @legendary-arena/server test` exits 0 (baseline 516 → 543 net-new tests). See [WP-209](WP-209-sweep-runs-server-storage-and-submission.md) + [EC-241](../execution-checklists/EC-241-sweep-runs-server-storage-and-submission.checklist.md). **Paired server WP to WP-210.** Lands the durable + visibility + invocation layer for the WP-194 + WP-195 sweep tooling that has been built-and-never-invoked since the WP-195 merge. New migration `018_create_sweep_runs.sql` creates `legendary.sweep_runs` (closed 6-column schema: `run_id text PRIMARY KEY`, `submitted_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now()`, `started_at timestamptz NOT NULL`, `cell_count int NOT NULL CHECK (cell_count >= 0 AND cell_count <= 10000)`, `anomaly_counts jsonb NOT NULL`, `manifest_blob jsonb NULL`; 1 BTREE index on `submitted_at DESC`). New `apps/server/src/sweep/` module mirrors `apps/server/src/analytics/` shape (WP-205 precedent): `sweep.types.ts` (imports `SweepAnomalyClass` + `SWEEP_ANOMALY_CLASSES` from `@legendary-arena/game-engine` — no redefinition, drift-by-import impossible) + `sweep.logic.ts` (3 async functions: `insertSweepRun`, `fetchLatestSweepRun`, `fetchRecentSweepRuns`) + `sweep.routes.ts` (POST + GET handlers + `registerSweepRoutes` export) + 2 test files (≥ 16 net-new node:test cases: ≥ 6 logic + ≥ 10 routes). **POST `/api/sweep/runs` auth (D-20702):** `guest` per D-9905 with shared-secret `X-Sweep-Token` header compared via `node:crypto.timingSafeEqual` (NOT `===` — timing-side-channel exposure forbidden); 401 + `{ data: [], error: 'unauthorized' }` returned BEFORE any DB I/O on mismatch. **GET `/api/sweep/latest` auth (D-20702):** `authenticated-session-required` per D-9905 with `SessionValidationErrorCode` collapse to `'unauthorized'` (D-10403 carry-forward). **Response envelopes:** POST success `{ data: { runId: string, accepted: true } }`; GET `{ data: { latest: SweepRunSummary, recentRuns: readonly SweepRunSummary[] } }` — intentional `data: object` deviation from WP-205's `data: readonly T[]` because the endpoint serves two semantically distinct payloads in one response. Status-code domains locked: POST `{201, 400, 401, 409, 413, 500}`; GET `{200, 401, 500}`. `Cache-Control: no-store` first-statement lock at every handler body (D-11504 carry-forward; grep gate ≥ 4 matches). POST body size cap 5 MB; cell-count cap 10000 (defense-in-depth against malformed payloads); `run_id` PRIMARY KEY enforces idempotent submission (duplicate → 409). `anomaly_counts` JSONB key validation lives at the route validator layer (CHECK constraint cannot enforce JSONB key sets in pure SQL); drift test asserts validator behavior matches `SWEEP_ANOMALY_CLASSES` byte-identical. **GitHub Actions nightly workflow** `.github/workflows/sweep-nightly.yml` cron `0 7 * * *` (07:00 UTC = midnight Pacific) runs `pnpm sweep:nightly` (new root script wrapping `node scripts/sweep-submit.mjs`). Submit script invokes `scripts/sweep-setup-matrix.mjs` (WP-194), classifies the manifest via `sweep.analyze.classifyManifest` (WP-195), POSTs the summary to `${API_BASE_URL}/api/sweep/runs` with `X-Sweep-Token`, then `rm -rf`'s the local `sweep-output/<run-id>/` directory on success (durable record now in DB; local artifact ephemeral per D-19403 carry-forward). **Render env var declaration:** `SWEEP_SUBMIT_TOKEN` `sync: false` in `render.yaml`; production server loud-fails at startup if unset (mirrors `ANALYTICS_USER_ID_SALT` D-20502 precedent). **API catalog update obligation (D-11804):** 2 new rows under `## Wired → Server-Registered Routes` per replace-whole-row merge semantics; each row cites D-20701 + D-20702 + relevant carry-forward D-entries. AND status = $expected`** — 0 rows ⇒ re-read ⇒ 404/409, no lost update; 409 off-table), `GET /api/handoffs/latest` (session-gated; deterministic `created_at DESC, report_id DESC` latest-report resolution, rows `ORDER BY finding_index ASC, handoff_id ASC LIMIT 500`; the chain view for the future dashboard). `branchRef`/`amendmentRequest` are references the server STORES, never actions it performs — the autonomous Builder (code→draft-PR) and Architect (spec amend) are deferred to a separately-gated follow-up. A new autonomous `POST /api/handoffs/verify` (additive trailing step in `inspection-nightly.yml` after `handoffs:sync`) reads the latest inspection report via `fetchLatestInspectionReport` and, for each `fix-proposed` handoff whose origin `reportId` differs from the latest (a genuine re-sweep ran post-merge), diffs its `(cellId, anomalyClass)` against the latest report: anomaly gone ⇒ `fix-proposed → resolved` (verified); anomaly present ⇒ `fix-proposed → claimed` (regression). Operator decisions (2026-06-10): reuses the WP-232 6-status lifecycle + transition table VERBATIM — **no new state** (Q4); **full-report diff**, not a targeted subset re-sweep (Q3); **post-merge / next-nightly** timing (Q2); the pipeline's first **autonomous-action** surface but **not** a code-writer (Q1). Server-authoritative via the existing `applyHandoffTransition` guarded UPDATE; the newer-report-only guard prevents false regressions; reuses the `X-Handoff-Token` (no new token, no migration). Additive-only on WP-232's handoff module. Reserves D-23301 (verification posture: full-report diff + newer-report guard + lifecycle reuse) + D-23302 (autonomous nightly verify step). 14 files (2 new + 5 modified source + 7 governance). Operator decisions (2026-06-10): sharded rotating ~2,120-cell budget (not full-per-run, not per-set-only); GitHub-hosted matrix shards (not self-hosted, not single-job); weekly full + keep daily smoke. - [x] WP-236 — Engine-Authoritative Start-of-Turn Draw (Auto-Draw + Once-Per-Turn Guard, Retire Draw Scaffold) — **Done 2026-06-11** (EC-266; baselines: engine build exit 0 + test 1182→1195 pass / 0 fail; arena-client build exit 0 + test 516 pass / 0 fail; sentinel fixture regenerated — `finalStateHash` aecc19→c08406, coherent-game HARD gate PASS [`outcome.winner` null unchanged, terminal `masterStrikeCount` unchanged in kind, `snapshotPerTurn.length` 2 unchanged]; `PRE_WP080_HASH` f2c9f9ec→85deb41a re-pinned; D-23601..D-23605 Active; EC_INDEX EC-266 Pending→Done folded in per RS-1). Engine rules-correctness fix for a **live bug on `play.legendary-arena.com`**: a player can draw more than once per turn. Sibling of WP-212 (which shipped the same once-per-turn pattern for `revealVillainCard`); fulfils the consolidated-engine-WP that D-10003 / D-10013 (Draw scaffold) explicitly named. Root cause: the engine `drawCards` move has no per-turn guard and no hand-size cap, so the only cap is the UI "Draw to 6" scaffold button (`TurnActionBar`) — and because moves are `client: false` / server-authoritative, the button's `handCount`-derived count is stale between click and frame, so rapid clicks (or any raw socket submission) over-draw. Fix: a new canonical `HAND_SIZE = 6` + pure `drawCardsIntoHand` helper (`moves/drawCards.logic.ts`, single draw primitive shared by the move, the rule-effect applier `applyDrawCards`, the play-phase `onBegin`, and the `runFixture` harness); the play-phase `turn.onBegin` auto-draws the active player's hand to `HAND_SIZE` **before** `onTurnStart` hooks (Magneto hand-trim must see the drawn hand); a new optional `G.hasDrawnThisTurn?: boolean` (mirrors `villainRevealedThisTurn` — optional, internal `G`, **not** UIState-projected) gates the kept `drawCards` move so a second submission is a zero-mutation no-op. The Draw scaffold button + `handCount` prop are deleted from `TurnActionBar` + both `PlayDesktop`/`PlayMobile` render sites; `ai.legalMoves` + `autoplay.mjs` stop emitting `drawCards`. The `drawCards` move + `CORE_MOVE_NAMES` + `UiMoveName` are **kept** (no contract/drift churn). **Operator scope decision 2026-06-10:** one-WP full auto-draw + scaffold retirement (over a surgical guard-only fix); accepts a regenerated sentinel replay (auto-draw is a behavioural change — `finalStateHash` + `messages` + `outcome` all shift; coherent-game sanity check replaces WP-212's byte-identity guard) + `PRE_WP080_HASH` re-pin. Exceeds the lint §5 ~8-file split guideline (operator-authorised; D-10012 envisions a consolidated WP). Reserves D-23601 (auto-draw + guard) + D-23602 (optional internal-`G` field) + D-23603 (Draw scaffold retirement) + D-23604 (fixture regeneration + hash re-pin) + D-23605 (draw-primitive consolidation). ~21 files (2 new + 16 modified source/test/fixture/server/client + 3 governance) across engine + server + arena-client. Depends on: WP-008A ✅, WP-009B ✅, WP-100 ✅, WP-158 ✅, WP-212 ✅. Out of scope: Reveal/Advance scaffold retirement (D-10011 / D-10012). See [WP-236](WP-236-engine-authoritative-draw.md) + [EC-266](../execution-checklists/EC-266-engine-authoritative-draw.checklist.md).

- [x] WP-277 — Registry Viewer Mechanic Dropdown: Fix List Scroll (Regression in WP-276) — **Done 2026-06-22** (EC-308; lightweight lane — bugfix; baseline `a8b076ad`). Operator-reported: the WP-276 dropdown list (134 mechanics, `overflow-y: auto`) **could not be scrolled** by wheel, scrollbar, or keyboard. **Root cause:** WP-276's `onViewportChange` was registered as a capture-phase `window` `scroll` listener (to close the `position: fixed` popover when the page/drawer scrolls so it doesn't drift from its anchor), but a capture-phase window scroll listener also receives scroll events from descendant scroll containers — so scrolling the popover's own list fired it and instantly closed the dropdown, making the list look unscrollable. **Fix:** `onViewportChange(event)` ignores scrolls whose `target` is inside the popover root (`rootEl.contains(target)`) and only closes on an outside page/drawer scroll or a resize (resize's target is the non-`Node` `window`, so it still closes). The list was always correctly overflowing (`scrollHeight 3957 > clientHeight 300`); only the close-on-scroll was the defect. **Executed:** reproduced live, fixed, re-verified in the preview (inner list scroll holds + popover stays open; outside scroll / resize / Escape still close; Berserk select → 49 cards); `typecheck`/`test`/`build` 0 (registry-viewer 86/86, predicate tests untouched). 1 viewer + 3 governance files; **no DECISIONS change** (a bugfix restoring WP-276's intended behavior — no new decision). `User-Visible Surface = cards.legendary-arena.com` (**D-24026 ✅ LIVE-VERIFIED 2026-06-22** — merged-main build against the live R2 feed: the list scrolls and holds while the popover stays open; outside scroll / resize / Escape still close). Hard-dep: WP-276 ✅. See [WP-277](WP-277-mechanic-dropdown-scroll-fix.md) + [EC-308](../execution-checklists/EC-308-mechanic-dropdown-scroll-fix.checklist.md).

Adds a **gallery filter mode**: a `loadoutGalleryActive` state + a final, **inert-when-off** narrowing stage in `applyFilters()` (mirrors the WP-270 mechanic stage) that restricts `filteredCards` to cards whose `extId` is in the shared draft's composition; `navigateToLoadoutGallery()` (mirrors `navigateToCard` — switch to Cards, clear other filters, narrow) + a dismissible inline banner (`Viewing loadout — N cards · ✕`). One-click entry from where the loadout already is: a "🖼 View as cards" button on the Loadout tab (`LoadoutBuilder.vue`, disabled on an empty composition) + a secondary action on the floating tray (`LoadoutTray.vue`), both emitting `view-as-cards`. Pure helper `loadoutGalleryCards.ts` (`compositionExtIdSet` skipping empty single slots + `for...of`; `isCardInLoadoutComposition` by `card.extId`, D-24018) carries the composition→member-card expansion (a hero group's member cards share the group `extId`) as the unit-tested invariant. **No new tab, no second grid (`CardGrid` untouched), no new LAGN loader, no "Custom LAGN" dropdown / account library** (deferred — needs identity/storage the public viewer lacks + re-introduces the multi-step flow). No engine/registry/server/contract/`MatchSetupConfig`/draft-logic/card-data change; consumes the existing `MatchSetupDocument["composition"]` + `FlatCard.extId`. ~9 files (3 modified components/wiring + 1 new helper + 1 new test; 4 governance). Reserves **D-24072**. `User-Visible Surface = cards.legendary-arena.com` (✅ D-24026 LIVE-VERIFIED 2026-06-25 — deployed Loadout tab renders the "🖼 View as cards" button). - [x] WP-306 — Setup-Contract Per-Field ext_id Validation (Henchman Id-Space Fix) — **Done 2026-07-04** (executed via `EC-336:` (impl `f84035af`) + `SPEC:` (governance close); baseline `origin/main` @ `031a91b9`; registry 130/0 [setupContract 21/0, +2 henchman cases], engine-runner 19/0, registry-viewer 110/0 + vue-tsc 0, `pnpm -r build` 0; no game-engine import in `packages/registry/setupContract` [grep clean]; D-24091 → Active; scope held to 9 code/test files — a 4th viewer stub `loadoutCardActions.test.ts` was an allowlist omission amended inline per 01.0b). **Standard two-session lane** (D-24028 — widens a locked `.types.ts` contract + spans 3 packages/apps; NOT lightweight). Fixed `validateMatchSetupDocument` (registry-side, browser-safe MATCH-SETUP validator, WP-091) which validated all five composition fields against ONE type-blind set built from `listCards().extId`. Over the **package** registry (whose `flattenSet` emits zero henchmen; used by the node / engine-runner path) that set contains **no** henchman-group `extId`, so it **false-rejected** a real `henchmanGroupIds` entry the engine's authoritative `validateMatchSetup` accepts (e.g. `core/sentinel`); and — being type-blind for every consumer — it **false-accepted** a villain id (`core/hydra`) in the henchman slot. Confirmed by probe + scaffold (2026-07-03). Load-bearing impact: `apps/engine-runner` (WP-304) used this validator as its sole scenario gate over the package registry, so a *correct* henchman scenario was rejected exit-2. The registry-viewer builder was **not** false-rejecting henchmen — its `flattenSet` already emits henchmen with `extId` (WP-122; D-24018) — so WP-306's only viewer-side effect is closing the type-blind false-accept. Fix: replace the global set with five per-field known sets — scheme/mastermind/villain/hero from `listCards()` by `cardType`; henchman from set data (`setData.henchmen[].slug`, mirroring the engine's `buildKnownHenchmanGroupQualifiedIds`, re-derived locally — no game-engine import). Widens `CardRegistryReader` (+`cardType`, +`listSets`, +`getSet`; the real registries already satisfy it — only test stubs change) and corrects the two engine-runner fixtures (`core/hydra`→`core/sentinel`). Closes the D-24018 "layer-2 = layer-3 id space" invariant for henchmen. 8 code/test files (3 registry, 2 viewer test-stubs, 3 engine-runner) + governance. Reserves **D-24091** (per-field validation; `CardRegistryReader` widen; henchman-from-set-data). **Distinct from WP-122** (viewer henchman `flattenSet` picker/filter fix — **Done 2026-05-01**, commit `a5c1653`; different file, does not touch the validator). `User-Visible Surface = none — dev/ops tooling + validation-only` (D-24026 N/A); `api-endpoints.md` N/A (no `apps/server` touch). - [x] WP-312 — Client Move-Acknowledgment Watchdog (Desync Auto-Recovery) (arena-client) — **Done 2026-07-05** (EC-341 impl commit + SPEC close; baseline `origin/main` @ `1a8ebba0`; move-ack watchdog in `bgioClient.createLiveClient` — arm on `submitMove` dispatch, ack on `_stateID` advance, fire WP-311 `resync()` on `MOVE_ACK_TIMEOUT_MS`=4000ms with no advance, `RESYNC_COOLDOWN_MS`=8000ms storm-guard, timers cleared on `stop()`; recovery never resubmits the lost move or pokes `_stateID`. arena-client suite **680/680** (5 new mock-timer watchdog tests), `vue-tsc` clean, `pnpm -r build` **0**; **D-24097 Active** (mechanism + locked values + D-10008 `client:false` dependency). **D-24026 live-verify operator-pending** on deploy. Drafted 2026-07-05). **Lightweight-lane eligible** (single layer arena-client; code surface = `bgioClient.ts` + its test; additive; no new contract; no determinism/hash impact). The **actual** fix for the recurring play.legendary-arena.com mid-match freeze that WP-309/WP-311 didn't fully close. Root cause (confirmed 2026-07-05 from Render logs, matches `bLCYAwi2tkp` + `B66jmk2QyP5`): a **connected desync** — the client drops one server `patch` (socket.io does not redeliver across a micro-reconnect), ends up one boardgame.io `_stateID` behind; the server then goes idle and **silently rejects** the client's stale moves (`ERROR: invalid stateID, was=[N] expected=[N+1]` → the master `return`s, sending the client nothing), and boardgame.io's patch-fail-sync fallback never fires (no next patch on an idle turn). `isConnected` never flips (socket alive) so WP-311's banner never shows. Because all engine moves are `client: false` (D-10008 — no client prediction), `_stateID` advances ONLY on a server frame, so "submitted a move but `_stateID` didn't advance" is a clean wedge signal. Fix: a **move-ack watchdog** in `createLiveClient` — on `submitMove`, record `_stateID` + arm a timer (`MOVE_ACK_TIMEOUT_MS` ~4s); a server frame advancing `_stateID` clears it; timeout with no advance → call WP-311's `resync()` (`stop()`+`start()` re-anchor), with a `RESYNC_COOLDOWN_MS` (~8s) storm-guard; timers cleared on `stop()`. Client-only — no engine/move/`G`/server change, no new state surface in `G`, no new dependency; recovery re-reads authoritative state via boardgame.io's own sync (never resubmits the lost move or pokes `_stateID`). §17 Vision (§3/§4/§11/§22 — no conflict; client read-only; determinism-preserving). §21 N/A (no HTTP route). Reserves **D-24097** (watchdog mechanism + locked timeout/cooldown + D-10008 dependency). `User-Visible Surface = play.legendary-arena.com` (D-24026 = a mid-match wedge auto-recovers within ~`MOVE_ACK_TIMEOUT_MS`, no reload). Hard-deps: WP-311 ✅ (resync + connection store), WP-090 ✅, WP-309 ✅, D-10008 ✅ (client:false). See [WP-312](WP-312-client-move-ack-watchdog.md) + [EC-341](../execution-checklists/EC-341-client-move-ack-watchdog.checklist.md).

- [x] WP-331 — HUD Turn Header Reads the Same Turn the Game Log Numbers By (Game Engine) — **Done 2026-07-08** (lightweight lane, single PR). On a completed match the HUD header showed `Turn 20` while the log's last line read `19.2.13` — an off-by-one the operator reported. Root cause: the match ends by transitioning `play → end`, and a phase change starts a fresh boardgame.io turn, so `ctx.turn` bumps one past the last real play turn; the `end` phase is `end: {}` (no `onBegin`), so `G.logMeta.turn` (which the log numbers by, WP-328/D-24114) stays frozen at the last play turn. Fix: `uiState.build.ts` `game.turn` reads `gameState.logMeta?.turn ?? ctx.turn` (was `ctx.turn`) — during live play identical (`logMeta.turn === ctx.turn`), so only the game-over display is corrected; `?? ctx.turn` fallback preserves lobby/setup + observation harnesses that skip `onBegin`. Engine `ui/*` suite **151/151** (2 new tests: game-over case + fallback), `pnpm -r build` 0. The lobby turn-base offset (log opening at `2.2.1` because the lobby is boardgame.io turn 1) is explicitly Out of Scope. §17 Vision (§14 observability / §11 read-only; no conflict; determinism N/A). §21 N/A. The D-24126 follow-up: `submitCompetitiveScoreImpl` step 2 now resolves the caller's own row via `findReplayOwnershipForAccount` + a secondary `findReplayOwnership` existence probe (keeps `replay_not_found` vs `not_owner`); removes the `accountId` compare; step 4 reads the caller's visibility; flow from the idempotency fast-path onward byte-unchanged; `findReplayOwnership` untouched. The D-24126-flagged follow-up. `submitCompetitiveScoreImpl`'s ownership check uses `findReplayOwnership(replayHash)` — a `LIMIT 1` query returning an ARBITRARY owner — so a match with **two authenticated seats** (both own the replay) can mis-reject a legitimate co-owner as `not_owner` (step 3) or read the wrong account's visibility (step 4). Today `[keyword:Spectrum]` is **unrecognized** → the 6 icon-bearing Spectrum hero lines fire **ungated** (a rules violation) and the 4 plain-English lines do nothing — the live 24-obs `spectrum`/`onPlay`/`parse-unrecognized` hollows on Silk cards (operator field report 2026-06-22, after the WP-273 wall-crawl fix cleared). Models Spectrum as a **conditional gate** — a new self-INCLUSIVE `distinctHeroClassesAtLeast` hero condition (≥3 distinct in-play hero classes; `SPECTRUM_CLASS_THRESHOLD = 3`) attached to the hook by a parser branch recognizing `[keyword:Spectrum]` (NOT a `HeroKeyword`) — and marks up the 3 simple gated effects (`quiver-of-thunderbolts`/`cascading-maneuver` draw; `long-range-spider-sense` `[keyword:reveal:2]` cost-lte). **Honest-partial:** `borrowed-cloaking-device`'s multi-card sum-cost-select reveal keeps an explicit placeholder marker → stays a reported hollow (named follow-up). ssw2-only (measured: 13 marker lines — 10 hero, ~3 villain-OUT). **Operator decision (2026-06-22): full gate** — gating the 6 ungated icon cards is the deliverable, not a regression. Determinism: the sentinel `finalStateHash` **re-pins** (the bot plays Spectrum cards; gating changes effects) — divergence EXPECTED, unlike WP-273/275. No new `HeroKeyword`; `heroKeywords.ts`/`apps/**`/`registry/**`/`server/**`/`ai.legalMoves.ts` byte-unchanged. Reserves **D-24055** (Spectrum = conditional gate; self-inclusive ≥3-class semantics + threshold) + **D-24056** (honest-partial scope). Hard-deps: WP-021/022 ✅, WP-179 ✅, WP-215/253 ✅, WP-257 ✅, WP-259/265 ✅. `User-Visible Surface = dashboard.legendary-arena.com/coverage` + in-game (the `spectrum` flip + a Silk-match diagnostics check; D-24026 live-verify post-deploy). RS-1 (scaffold-pinned at execution): the parse-to-condition, the `[keyword:reveal:2]` cost-lte token, the self-inclusive gate, the honest-fix proof, and the sentinel re-pin. RS-2: the ledger classification of a condition-gate mechanic. See [WP-280](WP-280-spectrum-conditional-keyword.md) + [EC-311](../execution-checklists/EC-311-spectrum-conditional-keyword.checklist.md).

Today villain deck/city cards are copy-indexed (`{set}-villain-{group}-{card}-NN`) while `cardStats`/`cardKeywords`/`villainAbilityHooks` key villains by the **definition** id (no `-NN`), and hero zone instances are slash-format (`{set}/{hero}/{card}#N`) while `heroAbilityHooks` key by the dash/slot FlatCard key — so villain `fightCost` (→0), `hasAmbush` (→false), villain `onFight`/`onAmbush` hooks, and hero abilities all **silently miss** at runtime. ### Backlog — Hero-Effect Observability & Authoring Follow-Ups (to be drafted)

> Parked candidates, **not yet drafted** — no WP file, no reserved number, no
> lint gate until drafting (the deferred-placeholder convention; numbers assigned
> at draft time). Each is its own small WP later. Surfaced 2026-06-29 from the
> X-Men "most effects didn't trigger" diagnostic (`gitSha 611e895`) and the
> observability packets that followed (decisions D-24081 message-log/hash
> separation + D-24082 play + condition-skip logging). **(Rows intentionally name
> no `WP-NNN` token so the `roadmap-counts.mjs` ledger parser skips them — they
> are backlog, not WPs; cross-references use D-numbers.)**

- [ ] **(backlog — to be drafted)** **Diamond Form over-fire (correctness bug)** (`core/emma-frost/diamond-form`) — the printed "Whenever you defeat a Villain or Mastermind this turn, you get +3[icon:recruit]" parses to an **unconditional onPlay** `recruit:3` because the setup parser reads the `[icon:recruit]` magnitude but not the "Whenever you defeat …" trigger prose — so the card grants +3 Recruit on play instead of on defeat. Needs a triggered/conditional representation (a defeat-scoped recruit, or a "defeated-a-villain/mastermind-this-turn" gate) rather than a flat onPlay effect. Verify against `keywords-full.json` / the printed card before encoding. Source: the X-Men observability diagnosis (decision D-24082 cluster).
- [ ] **(backlog — to be drafted)** **Hollow-prose hero card encoding** — core hero cards whose printed abilities are prose with **no machine marker**, so they parse to no executable effect and do nothing: `core/wolverine/healing-factor` (KO a Wound, then draw), `core/emma-frost/psychic-link` (each player may reveal another `[team:x-men]` Hero, then draws), `core/cyclops/determination` + `core/cyclops/optic-blast` (discard-to-play cost), `core/cyclops/unending-energy` (return-on-forced-discard). Encode each via the existing keyword / composition grammar (optional-KO-reward, team-reveal-draw, play-cost, return-on-discard) — an effect-authoring grind target off `/coverage`. Read the actual keyword defs + card corpus before picking the encoding (frequency ≠ tractability). Source: the X-Men observability diagnosis; the effect-authoring grind.
- [ ] **(backlog — to be drafted)** **Per-effect amount logging** — extend the observability logging (decision D-24082) into the hero effect handlers so **applied** effects emit amounts in the game log (drew N, +N Attack, +N Recruit, KO'd X), not just the play + condition-skip lines. The deferred half of the minimal observability pair. Cheap on the regression fixtures (the message log is excluded from `finalStateHash` per decision D-24081) but will re-pin the dedicated `messages` / `snapshotPerTurn[].messages` oracle. Source: the X-Men observability diagnosis (decision D-24082 §Consequence).

The **enabler** for the marketing username feature (marketing WP-033): the JS-readable `hanko` session cookie was host-scoped (WP-160/WP-241 call `register()` with no `cookieDomain`, adopting the SDK default per D-16002), so `www.` couldn't read a `play.` login. Both Hanko wrappers (`apps/arena-client/src/auth/hankoClient.ts` + `apps/dashboard/src/auth/hankoClient.ts`) now pass `cookieDomain: '.legendary-arena.com'` to `register()` **on production `*.legendary-arena.com` hosts only** — a new exported pure `resolveSessionCookieDomain(hostname)` (leading-dot suffix guard so `evil-legendary-arena.com` doesn't match; apex + subdomains → `.legendary-arena.com`; `localhost`/`*.pages.dev` → `undefined` so the browser doesn't drop the cookie and break dev/preview sign-in) read from `window.location.hostname` **only in the production factory** (tests inject `__hankoFactory`, never run it). No public-signature change; `cookieSameSite`/`storageKey` unchanged; no server/engine change. Result: **single sign-on across `*.legendary-arena.com`** (play + dashboard + www share one login); existing users re-sign-in once. arena-client `typecheck` 0 / `test` **797/797** (+5) / dashboard `typecheck` 0 / `test` **409/409** (+5) / `pnpm -r build` 0. §17 Vision (§14/§22; no conflict; determinism N/A). §21 N/A (no HTTP surface). - [x] WP-348 — Sign-out clears the `Domain`-scoped session cookie (fixes WP-347 regression) (arena-client + dashboard) — **Done 2026-07-09** (EC-378; standard lane — production auth regression, two apps; fresh worktree off `origin/main` @ `7a5bedfc`). **Operator-reported: "Sign out does nothing, header still shows I'm logged in as Jeff."** Root cause = a WP-347 regression: WP-347 set `cookieDomain: '.legendary-arena.com'` on `register()`, but `@teamhanko/hanko-frontend-sdk@2.6.0`'s `removeAuthCookie(){ O.remove(this.authCookieName) }` deletes the cookie by **name only, no `Domain`** (while `setAuthCookie` DOES use the domain) — a browser needs the matching `Domain` to delete a cookie, so `hanko.logout()` cannot remove the parent-domain `hanko` cookie and the session survives sign-out. Fix (shared wrapper, identical in both `hankoClient.ts`): a new `clearHankoSessionCookie()` expires the `hanko` cookie (`max-age=0`) `WITH domain=` from `resolveSessionCookieDomain` (the WP-347 rule) on production hosts + a bare host-scoped clear; `signOutCurrentSession` calls it in a **`finally`** after `logout()` (so a broker-down logout still completes the local sign-out, and the rejection still propagates unchanged). Guarded `typeof document/window === 'undefined'`. No public-signature / cookie-name / SameSite / server / engine change. arena-client `typecheck` 0 / `test` **811/811** (+2: clears-cookie + clears-on-logout-reject) / dashboard `typecheck` 0 / `test` **409/409** (byte-identical code; no jsdom there, existing test passes via the no-`document` guard) / `pnpm -r build` 0. §17 Vision (§14/§22; no conflict; determinism N/A). §21 N/A. Reserves **D-24140**. `User-Visible Surface = play.legendary-arena.com + dashboard.legendary-arena.com` (D-24026 = click Sign out → header returns to "Sign in", `hanko` cookie gone, reload doesn't re-auth; operator-pending on deploy). Hard-deps: WP-347 ✅, WP-160 ✅, WP-241 ✅. See [WP-348](WP-348-signout-cookie-domain-clear.md) + [EC-378](../execution-checklists/EC-378-signout-cookie-domain-clear.checklist.md).

Root cause: `computeFinalScores` (WP-020) uses flat `VP_VILLAIN=VP_HENCHMAN=1` / `VP_TACTIC=5` and never reads printed `vp` — because `G.cardStats` carries attack/recruit/cost/fightCost but **not** `vp` (the registry has `VillainCardSchema.vp` / `MastermindSchema.vp`, never plumbed into `G`). Because `computeFinalScores` also feeds `parScoring.logic.ts` (→ legends leaderboard), the understatement can **crown the wrong multiplayer winner**. Adds an immutable setup snapshot `G.cardVictoryPoints: Record<CardExtId, number>` (sibling to `cardStats`, built in `buildInitialGameState.ts` via a total/defensive `normalizePrintedVictoryPoints` — nullable `string|number`, `null`/NaN/non-int → omit) + `computeFinalScores` reads `G.cardVictoryPoints[cardId] ?? VP_<category>` per villain/henchman, `tacticVP = tacticsDefeated × (mastermind vp ?? VP_TACTIC)`; the flat constants **demoted to fallbacks** (values unchanged) so null-`vp` cards still score sanely. **Bystander stays 1, wound stays −1; `PlayerScoreBreakdown` shape/field-names unchanged** (value-only fix, no consumer break). Determinism: conditional-spread/omit-when-empty per WP-290 → `EMPTY_REGISTRY` fixtures stay byte-identical, sentinel re-pin execution-measured (expected none). Out of scope: no retro-rescore of historical DB rows, no PAR-weight change, no registry-schema change. ~4 code + 2 test + governance. - [x] WP-389 — Mastermind Base-Face Selection: Stop Silently Selecting Epic Faces (Game Engine) — **Done 2026-07-18** (EC-419; standard two-session lane, engine-only; drafted off `origin/main` @ `9c456412`; reserves **D-24193**). `findMastermindCards` (internal helper behind the exported `buildMastermindState`) assigns `baseCard = card` for EVERY non-tactic face with no early exit, so the **last** one wins — meaning any mastermind shipping both a base and an Epic face silently plays its **Epic** variant, which no player chose. Blast radius measured at draft: **65 masterminds across 24 sets** — 56 with an Epic variant, 9 with a transformation/second-form face (`wwhk` x6, `amwp` x3) — e.g. co2e Doctor Doom is selected as *Epic Doctor Doom*, attack `12+` instead of `10+`, and co2e Red Skull as *Epic Red Skull* `10+` instead of `7`. `core` is unaffected — one non-tactic face per mastermind — which is why no oracle caught it: with a single face, first-wins and last-wins are indistinguishable, so the loop was correct when written and diverged silently the first time a set shipped an Epic face; `baseCard` is always non-null and the `MastermindState` stays structurally valid, only its *content* is the wrong face. Fix is a single assignment guard (assign only while `baseCard` is null → **first** non-tactic face wins), retaining `tactic` as the discriminator per D-1413; tactic collection, the null-return guard, and `tacticsDeck` order are unchanged. **D-24193 (reserved):** the base card is the first non-tactic face; alternate faces become **unreachable**, and restoring them is TWO deferred features, not one — an Epic opt-in in the match-setup *envelope* (56; the 9-field composition lock stands) and in-match mastermind transformation (9). Also records an adjacent unfixed defect: 4 masterminds ship ZERO non-tactic faces and already resolve to the degenerate empty state. `wiki/master-strike.md` is corrected in the same commit (it asserted Epic faces are not engine-selectable); the WP-386 `// why:` comment needs NO edit — this fix makes its claim true as written. Determinism surfaces expected byte-identical (sentinel fixture + runtime-observed matrix are `core/dr-doom`, single-face); any drift = STOP, never re-pin. **Draft scaffold (observed, not argued):** with the guard applied the engine suite ran **1991 / 0 fail** (baseline-identical — no fixture encodes alternate-face values) and `sim:runtime-observed:check` reported the artifact current with **no regeneration**; tactic counts 4 -> 4. New test is non-vacuous: positive assertion on the base id AND a negative assertion that it is not the alternate id. 2 engine files + 4 governance. Baseline: engine suite **1991 pass / 464 suites / 0 fail**. Out of scope: any `epic` opt-in, registry schema change, card data, WP-388's strike resolvers. `User-Visible Surface = play.legendary-arena.com` (D-24026). Hard-deps: D-1413 ✅. See [WP-389](WP-389-mastermind-base-face-selection.md) + [EC-419](../execution-checklists/EC-419-mastermind-base-face-selection.checklist.md). **EXECUTED 2026-07-18** — exec worktree off `origin/main` @ `485db2eb`; engine suite **1991 → 1995 / 0 fail** (+4: first-face-wins with a negative not-Epic assertion, all tactics collected when a second non-tactic face is present [Epic never leaks into `tacticsDeck`], single-face resolution unchanged, zero-non-tactic-face still degrades to the fallback). **Guard-reverted control run: 1 fail** — the new assertion is non-vacuous, not a passing decoration. `pnpm -r build` 0; `sim:runtime-observed:check` current with **no regeneration**; sentinel `finalStateHash` + `PRE_WP080_HASH` byte-identical (both oracles pin `core/dr-doom`, single-face) — no re-pin. Ad-hoc face check recorded: `co2e/doctor-doom` → `Dr. Doom` `10+` (was *Epic Doctor Doom* `12+`), `co2e/red-skull` → `Red Skull` `7` (was *Epic Red Skull* `10+`), tactic counts 4 → 4. Scope = the 2 engine files + 5 governance. **D-24193 Active. Unblocks WP-388.** D-24026 live-verify operator-pending on deploy.

Engine suite **1995 → 2009 / 0 fail** (+14 covering AC-1..AC-9). **Control run with the four co2e dispatch branches removed: 8 fail** — the new assertions are non-vacuous. `pnpm -r build` 0; `sim:runtime-observed:check` current with **no regeneration**; sentinel `finalStateHash` + `PRE_WP080_HASH` byte-identical — no re-pin. `selectRedSkullKoTarget` now delegates to a shared `selectLowestCostHero` taking a **plain discriminator argument** (`'any' | 'team' | 'heroClass'` + slug), NOT a predicate callback (code-style §Functions); Red Skull behaviour byte-identical. - [x] WP-390 — Council Masterminds Resolve to an Empty Shell (Game Engine) — **Done 2026-07-19** (D-24206; WP-389 dependency cleared, then executed with the operator-chosen option 4 — Game.setup() THROWS rather than returning the degenerate empty MastermindState. Guard is scoped to `found but zero non-tactic faces`; mastermind-not-found / unparseable-id / narrow-test-mock all keep the pre-existing fallback, pinned by a dedicated test. Verified against REAL card data: all four councils throw with a diagnostic naming the mastermind, and core/magneto + co2e/red-skull + core/dr-doom still build with 4 tactics each. Engine 2039/2039, server 1016/0 fail. Supersedes WP-389 AC-4. Council modelling itself remains deferred — this only makes the gap unshippable) (no EC yet; design questions open, see packet; drafted off `origin/main` @ `485db2eb`; reserves no D-number yet). Four masterminds ship **ZERO** non-tactic faces, so `findMastermindCards` hits its `if (!baseCard) return null;` guard and `buildMastermindState` falls through to the degenerate empty state — the match runs against a mastermind with **no Master Strike, no tactics, no game text, and no abilities**. Affected: `2099/sinister-six-2099`, `2099/alchemax-executives`, `shld/hydra-high-council`, `shld/hydra-super-adaptoid` — i.e. **both** S.H.I.E.L.D. masterminds, so every `shld` gauntlet (both divisions, all counts) is currently played against nothing. Reachable content, not dead: `hydra-high-council` is referenced by `content/themes/aim-modok.json` + `hydra-uprising.json`, both `shld` boards are published in the live gauntlet catalog, and a published Gauntlet Guide covers it. **The data is not obviously wrong** — these are *council* masterminds whose four cards each carry their own `Master Strike:` + `[rule:Adapt]` line (hydra-high-council = Red Skull / Viper / Arnim Zola / Baron Helmut Zemo, four different strikes); the engine's one-base-face + N-tactics model simply does not decompose that way, so whether data, model, or both must change is the packet's first design question. Open questions before execution: which face leads; whether `[rule:Adapt]` must be implemented; whether `MastermindState` changes shape (a `G`-shape change would force the sentinel `finalStateHash` + `PRE_WP080_HASH` dual re-pin); and whether the smallest honest interim step is to make `Game.setup()` **throw** rather than silently produce an inert mastermind — a visibly rejected match beats one that looks fine and isn't. Flagged as a follow-up by WP-389 §Out of Scope, whose AC-4 pins today's zero-face behaviour — **so WP-389 must land first or its acceptance criterion needs rewriting.** Found 2026-07-18 while researching a strategy guide for the mastermind: the guide could not describe its threats because in play it has none. `User-Visible Surface = play.legendary-arena.com` (D-24026). Out of scope: the last-face-wins classifier (WP-389); `[rule:Adapt]` as a general keyword unless forced; card-data edits without a data-owner decision. See [WP-390](WP-390-inert-council-masterminds.md).
- [x] WP-395 — Canonical Villain & Henchmen Loadouts for Gauntlet Qualification (Registry + Server) — **Done 2026-07-20** (EC-435; drafted off `origin/main` @ `174b5df5`; reserves **D-24199**). Gives every mastermind a canonical villain-group + henchmen-group loadout sized per player count, and requires it for a replay to qualify as a **gauntlet leg**. **Casual play is unchanged** — free selection stays the game as printed; this is a ranked-surface constraint only (`legends.legendary-arena.com`), same class of precedent as D-24187's fixed-hero-pool division. **The argument is arithmetic, not taste:** `ScenarioKey` is `scheme::mastermind::sorted-villain-groups`, villain groups are unconstrained across all 41 sets (**134** groups, D-24131 §3 "any villain groups qualify"), and PAR is calibrated **per scenario key** with a validator rejecting `sampleSize < 500` — so against 639 scheme×mastermind leg pairs the space is 85,626 scenarios at 1p, 5.69M at 2p, 250.5M at 3–4p, and **8,205,239,889 at 5p (~4.1 TRILLION simulated games)**. A canonical loadout collapses that to **639 scenarios / ~319,500 games**. Since submission fail-closes on `par_not_published`, free villain choice does not make the ranked surface expensive — it makes PAR **unreachable**, and PAR gates every competitive score. **Migration cost is currently ZERO**: `legendary.competitive_scores` is empty (PAR never published), so nothing is grandfathered, re-keyed, or invalidated and no player loses an entry — this is the cheapest moment the change will ever have. **The cards already do most of it**: **103 / 111** masterminds declare `alwaysLeads` (Magneto → Brotherhood, Red Skull → HYDRA), so only the remaining 0–3 villain groups + henchmen (50 available) need authoring; 8 masterminds carry no `alwaysLeads` and need a hand-authored anchor. Hero selection stays free — heroes are not in `ScenarioKey`, so they cost nothing in calibration, which is exactly why D-24187's Open/Fixed-Pool divisions are untouched. Open forks for execution: where the loadout data lives (registry table beside `PLAYER_COUNT_SETUP` vs card data); how non-`alwaysLeads` groups are chosen (recommend thematic — defensible without the simulation this unblocks); **one canonical loadout (639) vs a small enumerated menu of three (1,917, still trivial, preserves real choice)**; discoverability of the requirement on the board + challenge link (the D-24186 / D-24190 failure class); and confirming `ScenarioKey` need not change shape. Sequence **ahead of any PAR publication work** — pointless after calibration begins, wasteful to calibrate before. WP-389 + WP-390 should land first so loadouts are authored against the mastermind players actually face. `User-Visible Surface = legends.legendary-arena.com` (D-24026). Out of scope: casual play; hero rules (D-24187); PAR calibration itself; hero-effect coverage. See [WP-395](WP-395-gauntlet-canonical-villain-loadouts.md). **Done 2026-07-20** — exec off `origin/main` @ `65ab7dbb`; registry 178/0, server 1024 (866 pass / 158 DB-skipped) /0, legends-board 82/0, `legends-board typecheck` 0, `gauntlet:loadouts:check` 0. 110 masterminds x 3 = 330 generated loadouts; qualification clause (g) in `getGauntletStandings`; migration 035 adds `competitive_scores.henchman_key` (henchmen are absent from ScenarioKey); board + challenge link publish the requirement. Scenario count corrected 1,917 -> **6,354** (per-count villain sizing). **D-24199 Active.**
- [x] WP-392 — Derive the published LAGN JSON Schema from the zod schema (Contract) — **Done 2026-07-18** (no EC; single-session contract packet; exec worktree off `origin/claude/wp-036-phase-b2-lagn-1-1`; **D-24196** now Active). Baselines: `packages/lagn-spec` suite **34 → 44 / 0** (+10); `pnpm --filter @legendary-arena/lagn generate:schema` then `git diff --exit-code -- schemas/` clean; `pnpm -r build` 0. Closes the hazard D-24193 recorded as known-and-unfixed: `src/validator.ts` maintained the format **twice** — `lagnSchema` (zod, labelled "Single Source of Truth") and `generateSchema()`, a hand-written JSON Schema literal that was *not* derived from it. The `lagn-schema-drift` CI job proves the committed JSON matches the **generator**, never that the generator matches **zod**, so the published contract could diverge with CI green — and had: `card_catalog.cards.items` and `replay.turns.items` were bare `{ type: 'object' }` against a 9-branch `z.discriminatedUnion` and a fully typed `TurnSchema`. **Demonstrated end-to-end at execution**: a bogus `card_type` passed the published schema and failed `validate()`; it now fails both. `generateSchema()` derives via `zod-to-json-schema` (runtime dep — `index.ts` computes `LAGN_SCHEMA` eagerly) with four locked, commented options: `target: 'jsonSchema7'` (the `2019-09` target emits the **draft-04 boolean** `exclusiveMinimum`, invalid under the declared 2020-12 URL), `removeAdditionalStrategy: 'strict'` (→ `additionalProperties: true`, matching zod's strip semantics; the library default `false` would reject documents `validate()` accepts), `$refStrategy: 'none'`, `effectStrategy: 'input'`. The hand-written literal is **deleted, not parked**. Post-derivation overrides sit *after* the spread (the library injects its own `$schema`) and re-apply only root draft URL / title / description / `$schema` default / `game_id` description — `lagn_version` stays the D-24193 `enum ["1.0.0","1.1.0"]`, pinned by test. **Unexpressible refinements are an enforced allowlist**: `UNEXPRESSIBLE_CONSTRAINTS` names all four (pool `mode`/`sets` + `ext_id` uniqueness; pool-sum-equals-count; `seq` +1 monotonicity; the 1.1.0 version gate) with path/constraint/reason, is embedded in the artifact as `x-lagn-unexpressible-constraints`, and is gated by a `ZodEffects` tree-walk count — an undocumented `.refine()` breaks the build (**mutation-tested red, then green**). - [x] WP-393 — Registry Version + Per-Set Content Hash Surface (Registry) — **Done 2026-07-20** (EC-423; standard two-session lane, registry-only; drafted off `origin/main` @ `06dda61d`; reserves **D-24197**). `CardRegistry` cannot say which snapshot of the card data it loaded: `RegistryInfo` is `{totalSets, totalHeroes, totalCards, loadedSetAbbrs, metadataBaseUrl}`, `SetIndexEntry` carries no version or hash, and `git grep registryVersion|content_hash|sha256` over `packages/registry/src` returns **nothing** (verified at draft). That makes the load-bearing half of WP-394's provenance work — "which registry snapshot generated this LAGN?" — unanswerable, and the LAGN side must not reach up into registry internals to compute it. Adds **optional** `registryVersion?: string` + `setContentHashes?: Record<string,string>` (keyed by set **abbr**, values `sha256:<hex>`) to `RegistryInfo` — optional because `apps/registry-viewer` vendors its own structurally-independent copy, so required fields would drift it silently with `pnpm -r build` still green, computed in **both** loaders over the **RFC 8785 canonical serialization of the parsed set object** — not raw bytes, so the HTTP and local paths agree byte-for-byte for identical data; `registryVersion` derives from the hashes of the sets **actually loaded**, sorted by abbr — load-**order**-independent but deliberately load-**scope**-dependent (a consumer needs what the producer saw), so it is NOT a global snapshot id; the authoritative per-set evidence is `setContentHashes` (pinned by AC-5). New shared `canonicalJson.ts` (JCS) is the contract both this WP and WP-394 hash against. Guardrails: registry imports Node built-ins + `zod` only; hashes only on the public surface (never raw JSON/buffers); both loaders change together or it's a FAIL; no `.reduce()` in the accumulation. §17 Vision (§22 determinism — a reproducible version string). Out of scope: any LAGN change, card data, DB persistence, HTTP publication, consumer wiring. `User-Visible Surface = none — infrastructure`. **Unblocks WP-394.** Hard-deps: none. See [WP-393](WP-393-registry-version-content-hash.md) + [EC-423](../execution-checklists/EC-423-registry-version-content-hash.checklist.md). **Done 2026-07-20** — exec worktree off `origin/main` @ `948b1b0e`; registry suite **148 → 171 / 0** (+23: RFC 8785 conformance incl. the spec's own number and string vectors, loader parity, order-independence, scope-dependence, zero-scope omission, fresh-copy); `pnpm -r build` 0; `registry-viewer` `vue-tsc` clean with **both** vendored `RegistryInfo` copies untouched (AC-8 — run with the UNSCOPED `--filter registry-viewer`, since the scoped form matches zero projects and passes vacuously); forbidden-import grep clean; 11-file scope exact. **D-24197 Active.** Fields are OPTIONAL by design (the viewer vendors two independent copies; required fields would drift them silently with the build still green). `registryVersion` is load-**scope**-dependent, not a global snapshot — pinned by AC-5. Zero-set scope omits both fields rather than emitting a digest over nothing (AC-5b). **Unblocks WP-394.**

Lets a LAGN document answer *"which card effect does this replay reference, and can I verify it without the registry?"* via optional, hash-anchored provenance: `catalog_ref` (pins the producer's registry load scope — not a global snapshot; see D-24197), `registry_ref` (`ext_id` + `face_id`, stable ids only — **replaces JSON pointers**, which break on array reorder), `effect_snapshot` (frozen **evidence**, explicitly not authoritative), and `image.uri`. The other two are **SATISFIED**: the LAGN 1.1.0 seam (EC-422 / D-24195) and the derived-schema gate (**WP-392** / D-24196, PR #828) both merged 2026-07-18. See [WP-394](WP-394-lagn-1-2-0-provenance.md) + [EC-424](../execution-checklists/EC-424-lagn-1-2-0-provenance.checklist.md). **Done 2026-07-20** — exec worktree off `origin/main` @ `4df8bfbd`; lagn suite **44 → 54 / 0**; all **five** fixtures validated against the generated JSON Schema via `ajv` 2020-12 AND zod; `generate:schema` + `git diff --exit-code -- schemas/` clean; `pnpm -r build` 0; 14-file scope. **D-24198 Active.** **Empirical scaffold RUN before implementing** (01.4): prototyping the version gate produced 42/2 — both failures the expected mechanical consequences (the `ZodEffects` allowlist count and the regenerated schema), **zero existing fixture breakage**, so nothing was folded into scope. `LAGN_VERSION` stays **1.1.0** — readers accept 1.2.0, writers keep stamping 1.1.0, so no producer emits provenance and `migrateToCurrent` still targets 1.1.0 (the 1.1.0→1.2.0 step is registered but **unreachable**; a 1.2.0 input returns unchanged with `applied: []`). **§21 TRIGGERED** — the acceptance envelope narrowed (pre-1.2.0 body carrying provenance: `201` → `400`), so `POST /api/me/loadouts` (the only `validate`-gated request row) was replaced WHOLE per D-11804; `PATCH` carries no `lagn` and is untouched. Two new refinements recorded in `UNEXPRESSIBLE_CONSTRAINTS`, count gate **mutation-tested red→green**. **Defect found and fixed in-flight:** zod's `.startsWith('sha256:')` derives the JSON Schema pattern `^sha256\:`, an invalid Unicode-mode escape that made `ajv` throw compiling the published schema — replaced with `^sha256:[0-9a-f]{64}$`, which is also the stricter constraint. - [ ] WP-396 — `bystanderLost` Has No Producer (Game Engine / Scoring) — **Draft — design question open** (no EC yet; drafted off `origin/main` @ `15b4cc06`; reserves no D-number yet). `deriveScoringInputs` hardcodes `const bystanderLostCount = 0` with a D-4801 safe-skip comment naming this WP as the follow-up, so the penalty the scoring model ranks **highest** multiplies by a constant zero and a match is never scored on civilian casualties. Not cosmetic: `validateScoringConfig` **hard-fails** any scenario config violating three structural invariants — `bystanderReward > villainEscaped`, `bystanderLost > villainEscaped`, `bystanderLost > bystanderReward` — so every config that ever ships is *required* to weight this term above both an escape and a rescue, encoding VISION §21 in code against a term that cannot fire. **Two defects, not one.** (1) Nothing counts it — `ENDGAME_CONDITIONS` has no `BYSTANDERS_LOST` member; but the count is **already computed** at `villainDeck.reveal.ts:242–250`, which measures the supply-pile delta across the escape branch, uses it for a log line, and discards it. (2) The state semantics disagree — `resolveEscapedBystanders` returns an escaped villain's bystanders **to the supply pile** (`// why: ... prevent memory leaks and bystander depletion`), so today they are recycled rather than lost; counting that delta as `bystanderLost` while the same bystanders re-enter supply would make penalty and board state contradict each other. **The rulebook contradicts the engine**: `docs/legendary-universal-rules-v23.md:565-569` says "Put the captured/rescued Bystanders in the Escaped Villains/Overrun pile" (reinforced at `:1544-1548` — Hidden Witnesses "stay in the Escape Pile"), and additionally requires **each player to discard a card** for failing to rescue them, which the engine does not do either. So returning to supply is a rules divergence, not an MVP simplification, and this WP is "make the escape branch resolve correctly, then count what it produces" rather than "add a counter". Destination + discard-penalty scope is a **rules-owner** call and **blocks execution**. **Sequencing argument — land before PAR calibration publishes.** `computeParScore` sets `bystanderLost: 0` explicitly and `ParBaseline` has no bystanders-lost field (`roundsPar` / `bystandersPar` / `victoryPointsPar` / `escapesPar` only), so PAR structurally assumes zero casualties. If baselines publish while the producer is inert and it lands afterward, Raw Score gains a term PAR has no counterpart for, every score shifts worse against an unchanged baseline, and the fix is either a `scoringConfigVersion` bump (VISION §22: breaks comparability with all historical entries) or re-calibrating baselines VISION §22 declares immutable. PAR is unpublished and `legendary.competitive_scores` is empty **today** — that window closes on publication. Open questions: lost-vs-returned (blocking); counter vs structured event log (D-4801 locked "read G directly" and rejected an event-log dependency, so a `G.counters` member follows the `ESCAPED_VILLAINS` + `?? 0` precedent); whether `ParBaseline` gains a `bystandersLostPar` field (breaking change to a persisted version-pinned type); whether non-escape loss paths exist (scheme twists, tactics, `[rule:Adapt]`). Found 2026-07-18 while correcting `wiki/scoring.md`, which claimed this penalty was "counted from bystander-resolution paths" — it is not counted from anything; the same claim had propagated into a published gauntlet guide telling readers a run that bleeds civilians scores worse. Docs corrected (this repo #822/#829, `legendary-arena-website#76`); the engine gap is this WP. `User-Visible Surface = play.legendary-arena.com`, `legends.legendary-arena.com` (D-24026). Out of scope: the other three inert penalties (`schemeTwistNegative` / `mastermindTacticUntaken` / `scenarioSpecificPenalty` — same root cause, own design questions, own WPs; the PAR-window argument applies to all four); PAR re-calibration (WP-049); any `scoringConfigVersion` bump (governance, not implementation); retro-fixing published scores (none exist). No code dependency on WP-389/WP-390, though WP-390 leaves both `shld` masterminds inert so those gauntlets cannot exercise mastermind-driven capture end-to-end. See [WP-396](WP-396-bystander-lost-producer.md).

- [x] WP-367 — Deck-Exhaustion Final Turn → Tie (Game Engine) — **Done 2026-07-12** (EC-397; isolated worktree off `origin/main` @ `e38f0314`; design per **D-24159** / **D-24160** / **D-24161** now Active). Implements the rulebook deck-exhaustion end condition Jeff reported: the moment `G.villainDeck.deck` **or** `G.heroDeck` reaches zero, a **sticky** `FINAL_TURN_TRIGGERED` latch is set (via `game.ts` `turn.onMove` → new pure `endgame/finalTurn.logic.ts`); the current turn is played out as a last chance to win/lose; at `turn.onEnd`, if latched AND `evaluateEndgame` is still null, `FINAL_TURN_TIE` is set → `evaluateEndgame` returns the new **`tie`** outcome (ranked LAST, after all win/loss). The latch **never** ends the game on its own and a card effect that refills a deck does **not** cancel the final turn (D-24159). The **villain deck no longer reshuffles from its discard** — exhaustion is terminal (D-24160; `shuffleDeck` import dropped; sentinel unchanged since the reshuffle path is dead in practice — villain cards route to City/piles, never the villain discard). New optional `UIState.finalTurn?` projection (omit-when-absent, zero fixture churn) carries the HUD warning data; the tie surfaces through the existing `gameOver` projection. **Competitive scoring maps `tie → null`** (no gauntlet leg; `CompetitiveOutcome` + column + drift test unchanged — D-24161); fixture + sweep `winner` validators accept `'tie'`; `MatchSnapshotOutcome.result` typed `EndgameOutcome`. Baselines: `pnpm --filter @legendary-arena/game-engine build` 0; engine suite **1922/447/0** (+16: evaluator tie/rank/latch-null, finalTurn.logic latch+resolve sticky/idempotent, uiState finalTurn projection, no-reshuffle); `pnpm -r build` 0; `arena-client` typecheck (vue-tsc) 0 + test **884/884**; engine replay-fixture tests green so the sentinel `finalStateHash` is unchanged (reshuffle path dead in the sentinel game); `sim:coverage --check` OK. §17 Vision (§3/§22/§24/§25/NG-1; determinism preserved — reshuffle removal subtracts an RNG source). §20 N/A (game-rules change; no funding surface). §21 N/A (no HTTP endpoint; competition change is internal to an existing library function, not a catalog entry). **Follow-on (deferred):** a dedicated "final turn" warning **banner UI** in arena-client rendering `UIState.finalTurn` (client-only WP); competitive/ranked treatment of a tie beyond `null` (ranked-design WP). `User-Visible Surface = play.legendary-arena.com` — **D-24026 operator-pending on deploy** (in a real match, exhaust a deck → "final turn" log line, then a tie result on the endgame screen if no one wins). Hard-deps: WP-010 ✅, WP-014A ✅, WP-135 ✅, WP-067 ✅. See [WP-367](WP-367-deck-exhaustion-final-turn-tie.md).

- [x] WP-402 — LAGN 1.3.0: Hero Alternates (Contract) — **Done 2026-07-20** (EC-437; standard two-session lane, contract package; drafted off `origin/main` @ `1c8809f3`; **D-24210** + **D-24211** Active). Adds optional `setup.hero_alternates` so a saved or shared loadout carries **bench heroes** — the operator's "seven-hero shortlist, five played, two in reserve" — instead of losing the reserves at the first round trip. **The bench cannot be extra entries in `setup.heroes`:** hero count is exact-enforced against `PLAYER_COUNT_SETUP` (**3** at 1p, **5** at 2–4p, **6** at 5p — `playerCountSetup.ts:47-53`, enforced at `matchSetup.validate.ts:496` and in `setupContract.validate.ts`), and the viewer maps `setup.heroes[].id` straight into `heroDeckIds`, so seven entries produce an invalid draft *and* a thrown `Game.setup()`. The bench is therefore additive to whatever the seat count requires — **nothing hardcodes 5, 7, or 2**. Copies the D-24195 `support_pools` shape decisions verbatim: optional, version-gated, strict superset, sibling of what it relates to. **The version gate is the load-bearing half** — `lagnSchema` is not `.strict()`, so an ungated bench written into a 1.1.0 document is silently STRIPPED on parse and the preset returns empty, the worst available failure mode; a pre-1.3.0 document carrying one is rejected instead. **Fixes a latent contract defect found by the draft-time scaffold**: the provenance gate is written as `data.lagn_version === LAGN_VERSION_1_2_0`, so the moment 1.3.0 joins `LAGN_SUPPORTED_VERSIONS` a 1.3.0 document carrying `catalog_ref` is **rejected** — by a message reading "requires lagn_version 1.2.0 **or later**". Measured, not reasoned: equality gate → `INVALID`, ordinal gate → `VALID`. **D-24211** locks version gates as ordinal. **Empirical scaffold RUN at draft** (01.4): baseline **54 / 0** → scaffolded **52 / 2**, both failures mechanical (the pinned `lagn_version` enum assertion + schema regeneration), **zero existing fixture breakage**, refinement-count gate green at +2 nodes / +2 allowlist entries (1:1 confirmed) — nothing needed folding into scope. **No `.max()` on the bench**: a cap in a published open standard cannot be relaxed without a major version, and the two-slot affordance is a UI decision (WP-404). `LAGN_VERSION` **stays 1.1.0** — readers accept 1.3.0, writers do not emit it, so no payload moves here. **§21 TRIGGERED**: the acceptance envelope moves (a body carrying `hero_alternates` is stripped-and-accepted today, rejected after), so `POST /api/me/loadouts` is replaced WHOLE per D-11804. **D-24210** records the boundary the whole arc rests on — the bench is loadout metadata and **never gameplay state**; in-match substitution was considered and declined, since it would break the `MatchSetupConfig` 9-field composition lock and invalidate D-24119's assumption that `initialState` fully describes the match. Out of scope: the writer flip, any `apps/*` change, the MATCH-SETUP envelope, any engine change, any bench cap. `User-Visible Surface = none — infrastructure`. Hard-deps: WP-394 ✅, WP-392 ✅, D-24195 ✅. Fixed to compare version positions (same D-24211 principle, second site), keeping the step genuinely unreachable until WP-404 flips the writer. `LAGN_VERSION` stays 1.1.0 (AC-6/AC-10); `package.json` untouched; no registry dependency (AC-11). §21 handled — `POST /api/me/loadouts` row replaced WHOLE. - [x] WP-411 — Set `ctx.gameover` at End of Game (Top-Level `endIf`) (Game Engine) — **Done 2026-07-21** (EC-446; standard two-session lane; **D-24223** Active). **Critical latent bug found while live-verifying WP-407/408:** a clean production win (`J8rw6ziU4Xg`, Magneto's four tactics defeated) returned `404 match_not_finished` from `result-lagn`; a DB read showed **both `metadata.gameover` AND `state.ctx.gameover` null** after a decisive victory. Root cause (verified): the endgame check is a **phase-level `endIf`** on the `play` phase — which in boardgame.io only **ends the phase** (→ the empty `end` phase) and does **not** set `ctx.gameover`. `LegendaryGame` has **no top-level `endIf`** and no `events.endGame()` anywhere (both greps empty); the phase-`endIf` comment even states the mistaken belief that a truthy phase-`endIf` return becomes `ctx.gameover` (true only for a **top-level** `endIf`). So **no match ever registers gameover** — which is why every completed-match consumer is dead in prod: competitive submission (WP-338 → `competitive_scores` **empty**), result-LAGN (WP-406), Hall of Legends (WP-407/408), match summaries (D-24169); every "finished match" query returns 0 rows. **No test caught it** because the suite asserts the pure `evaluateEndgame(G)` function, never the boardgame.io wiring that turns it into `ctx.gameover`. **Fix:** add a top-level `endIf: ({ G }) => evaluateEndgame(G) ?? undefined` to `LegendaryGame` (sets `ctx.gameover` → boardgame.io persists `metadata.gameover`), remove the redundant/incorrect `play`-phase `endIf`, **keep the four-phase structure** (lobby/setup/play/end — pinned by `game.test.ts`). **Tests close the gap:** a `game.test.ts` unit assertion on `LegendaryGame.endIf` + an `engine-runner` integration test that plays a match to a **real** win/loss and asserts `ctx.gameover` is set. **Determinism (scaffold-gated):** setting `ctx.gameover` mutates no `G`, so `finalStateHash` is unchanged for an identical `G`; the only exposure is that games now **terminate** at the win/loss point — any fixture recorded *past* it is re-recorded via the canonical recorder + reasoned re-pin (never hand-edited), never a silent shift (AC-4). No server edit (the server already reads `metadata.gameover`). §21 N/A. **`User-Visible Surface = none directly — engine wiring`, but it unblocks the completed-match pipeline — D-24026 live-verify REQUIRED** (a real completed match now sets `metadata.gameover` and returns a `result-lagn` document; this simultaneously unblocks the WP-407/408 populated-roster verifications). Hard-deps: **WP-304 ✅** (engine-runner harness). See [WP-411](WP-411-top-level-endif-gameover.md) + [EC-446](../execution-checklists/EC-446-top-level-endif-gameover.checklist.md). **Done:** top-level `endIf` added, phase `endIf` removed; the AC-3 wiring test (framework sets `ctx.gameover` via boardgame.io's reducer) was realized in `game.test.ts` — the engine-runner's `runSimulation` harness bypasses boardgame.io and structurally cannot observe `ctx.gameover`, so `runMatch.test.ts` got an honest end-to-end termination smoke instead (EC-446 §Execution Amendment). Baselines: build 0; game-engine 2053→2055/0 (+AC-1 endIf unit, +AC-3 reducer wiring); engine-runner 19→20/0; whole-repo `--no-bail` clean. **Determinism (AC-4):** `finalStateHash` + `PRE_WP080_HASH` **unchanged** — no `G` mutation, no fixture recorded past the win/loss point; re-record was a verified no-op. **AC-6 (D-24026 live-verify) pending the engine deploy.**

- [x] WP-414 — Bot-Ally Stall Surfacing + Restart Revival (Server) — **Done 2026-07-22** (EC-449; standard two-session lane; **D-24229, D-24230** Active; fast-follow to WP-375). **Reported prod freeze:** a bot-ally match on `play.legendary-arena.com` froze on the bot's turn while the operator ran concurrent sessions that merge to `main` (→ Render redeploy → the in-process `BotAllyDriver` — `botAllyDriver.mjs:47` — is destroyed mid-match). A **live** driver can never freeze forever (it faults within `BOT_MAX_MOVE_STEPS_PER_TURN`), so a lasting freeze means the driver is **gone** (restart) or was persisted `faulted` and never revived — and `rehydrateBotAllyDrivers` today revives only `status='active'` rows, while `fault_message` is written to the side-table but **no endpoint exposes it**: the human on seat 0 waits forever with no signal. **Fix (server half):** (a) a read-only `GET /api/match/:matchId/bot-ally-status` (`guest` by capability, side-table-only — never the bgio blob, D-24095) → `{ driving, status, message }`, `absent` for a non-bot match; (b) the driver retries a wedged turn **once** from a fresh fetch before faulting; (c) restart revival re-attaches a **still-live** `faulted`/`exhausted` match, bounded by a new `revive_count` column (`MAX_REVIVALS=3`) so a genuinely-stuck match settles to a *surfaced* `faulted` instead of looping every deploy. Migration `036` adds `revive_count` (additive/idempotent). **No `server.mjs` edit** (route registers inside `registerBotAllyRoutes`; revival is already wired). §21 APPLIES (new `guest` `GET` → `api-endpoints.md` row). `User-Visible Surface = play.legendary-arena.com` — **D-24026 REQUIRED** (a bot-ally match survives a restart and keeps playing; a wedged one settles to a surfaced fault). Pairs with **WP-415** (the client banner that consumes the status surface). Hard-deps: **WP-375 ✅**, **WP-309 ✅**, **WP-115 ✅**. See [WP-414](WP-414-bot-ally-stall-surface-and-revival.md) + [EC-449](../execution-checklists/EC-449-bot-ally-stall-surface-and-revival.checklist.md). **Done:** `GET /api/match/:matchId/bot-ally-status` registered inside `registerBotAllyRoutes` (guest, side-table-only, `{200,500}`, `absent` 200 for a rowless match); the within-turn retry wraps the whole turn as `driveBotTurn` → `attemptBotTurn` (`retriedOnce`-guarded single re-attempt covering all four fault exits); `readActiveBotAllyMatches` → `readRevivableBotAllyMatches` (active OR under-cap faulted/exhausted, `MAX_REVIVALS=3`), revival marks gone/gameover matches `completed` and flips a non-active revived row `active` + `revive_count += 1` via `markBotAllyMatchRevived`; migration `036` additive/idempotent; **no `server.mjs` edit**; `api-endpoints.md` row added. Baselines: `pnpm -r build` 0; server bot-ally suites 41/0 (+7 status/revival/retry). **AC-6 (D-24026 restart-survival + surfaced-fault) — data-level VERIFIED 2026-07-23** (restart-survival evidenced by match `SbknquXtOwY` reaching `revive_count=3` while advancing turn 23→24 across restarts; surfaced-fault/stopped signal confirmed via live `GET /api/match/:id/bot-ally-status`); only the on-screen render eyeball remains. Unblocks **WP-415** (client banner).
